12.11.2024 10:55 - Sudenaz Hnm: Dwaine Chesmain İnsurance
12.11.2024 10:56 - Sudenaz Hnm: (İnsurance taslagi olarak kullanalim?)

Klinik gereklilik (sf1) (sigortalarin hepsi yapilan her islemin  her bir dis dahil kozmetik olmadiginin kanıtını istiyor:;

➡️ Her implantin yeri vegerekliligi
➡️ Graftlar icin indikasyon
❌ Kron gerekliligi (bu hastada yoktu ama eklenecek)
➡️Gecici dis gerekliligi

Sf2: implant yerlestirmeleri

Sf:3 x-ray

⚠️ Dokumanlar non amendeble (degistirilemez) yani orbis tarafindan sifrelenmis, fiziksel imzali, e imzali, pdf sertifikali olnali

⚠️İnsurance icin her zaman x ray isteniliyor, before ve after, (bazilari tarih ile), bunlar pdfe dahil olmali ek olarak da gonderilmeli 

(X rayde kamera fotoğraflari kabul edilmiyor. X ray aldiktan sonra foto alip bilgisayarda ss tuşuna basarsaniz orbis bilgisayarlarina x ray gidiyor.)

15.11.2024 10:13 - Sudenaz Hnm: Herkese günaydın şu FAQ sorularına eklemek istediğiniz veya değiştirmek istediğiniz yerler var mıdır? 

1. When and how will I be informed about my next transfer to the clinic?
We inform our patients via WhatsApp, typically messaging them in the evening. While we aim to send transfer details as early as possible, it may take until 11 PM for us to confirm your transfer time.

2. How much time will my procedure take?
The duration of your procedure varies depending on the treatment. Once your appointment is confirmed, we’ll provide an estimated timeframe if possible.

3. What should you bring with you to the clinic?
We recommend bringing your passport on your first visit. It’s also helpful to have a digital copy of your passport and know your passport number before your flight.

4. Can you use our transfers for different locations while you are here?
Our transfers are designed to take you between your hotel and the clinic. After your procedure, you can request to be dropped off at a different location. For any additional transfer needs, please contact us. These services may incur extra charges.

5. What should you eat/drink before coming to the clinic?
For procedures involving sedation, refrain from eating or drinking for at least 6 hours before your appointment. For all other visits, we recommend having a good breakfast, particularly on your first day.

6. Will I be sedated?
Sedation is not standard unless specifically requested. If you would like sedation, please inform us in advance. This service incurs additional costs and requires you to fast for 6 hours beforehand. Rest assured, even without sedation, our procedures are pain-free.

7. What about my medical history?
We will discuss any specific health issues with you before your arrival. At the clinic, you’ll complete a medical history and allergies form. Your health status will always be carefully considered before starting any treatment.

8. How will I know when and where my airport transfer will pick me up?
This information is included in the voucher document sent via email. Typically, airport transfers pick you up 3–3.5 hours before your flight. Upon arrival, our transfer driver will be waiting for you.

Tip for departure day: We recommend checking in for your flight online to save time at the airport.

9. What should I do if I encounter any issues with my hotel?
For any hotel-related concerns, please consult us before speaking to the hotel staff. We’re here to assist you.

10. What can I expect regarding temporary and permanent teeth?
During your visit, we’ll provide detailed aftercare guidelines based on your treatment type, whether you’re receiving temporary or permanent restorations.

11. Why didn’t I get any messages about my next appointment’s transfer?
In some cases, your next appointment might not be scheduled for the following day, but rather with a one-day gap. If you haven’t received a message by the morning of the day in question and are unsure whether you’re scheduled to visit the clinic, feel free to contact us for clarification.

12. How long does the trip from the hotel to the clinic take?
The hotels we work with are generally within a 15-minute drive to the clinic. If there’s any variation in this distance, we will inform you, and your transfers will be arranged accordingly.

13. How will I get my prescription?
If you’re prescribed medication, we’ll ask the transfer driver to stop by a pharmacy to collect it. You’ll receive a prescription guideline for your reference, which you should keep, while the actual prescription will need to be handed to the pharmacist. If you have any questions or issues, don’t hesitate to reach out to us.

6.12.2024 20:17 - Sudenaz Hnm: Arkadaşlar merhaba, evrakların çoğunu kasım ayındaki deneyimlerimize göre güncelledim biraz da estetikleri ile oynadım aynı zamanda  dokümanarın drive'daki yerini değiştirdim. (Eskiden hasta klasörleri arasındaydı) 

Dokümanların elde kalmaması durumunda bu klasörü kullanarak çıkartabilirsiniz. *(Her birinizin bilgisayarı yazıcıya bağlı ve doküman çıkarmaya hazır.)* 

Drive Doküman Klasörü:
https://drive.google.com/open?id=15PadR7w4FfQGX2c0Cy3t7hKXnbFr4kSq&usp=drive_fs

Ne değişti?
Görsel iyileştirmeler, PDF içinde doldurulabilirlik 
yazım hatası ve görünüm düzeltmeleri 
- font değişti

Satisfaction form: 
- Her visitte alınacak
- Satisfaction form hakkında hastaların visit sayısına göre yaşadığı kafa karışıklığı düzeltildi
- Satisfaction form artık implant, all on x, kaplama, visit sayısı fark etmeksizin verilebilir *(Visit 1,2, hatta rpt de dahi verelim.)* 
- Satisfaction form'da sadece *diş rengi* doldurtuyorduk, artık design da var. 
- Hastaların alakasız yerlere isim yazmasını engellemek adına her boşluğa ne yazılacağını anlatan silik yazılar eklendi. 
- Ödemenin ne zaman yapılması gerektiğine dair hem satisfactionda hem de welcome consentte daha açıklayıcı bilgilendirme 

Welcome Consent:
- Ödemeye dair önemli bilgilendirmeler eklendi.
- Her visitte alınacak. 

Diğer belgeler: 
- Daha açıklayıcı ve yönlendirici bilgilendirmeler, yeni belgeler *sedasyon, ameliyat sonrası, beyazlatma, all-on-x, vb.*

Alt cene all on 6 + tek çene plak

Hasta otelinin 3 gecesini kendisi ödedi, 4 gece sadece ilk vizite özel biz karşılıyoruz. Ikinci vizitte klinige yok yakın bir yerde otel rezervasyonu yapacak. Kliniğe yürüme mesafesinde hastaya pazartesi itibari ile klinik otel arasi transfer vermiyoruz. 

Hastadan ilk visit ödemesi olarak 2000 gbp kart ile ödeme alındı. 

Hasta ikinci vizitte nakit ödeme yapacak. 1850 Gbp nakit getirmezse 2.050 Gbp ödeme alinacak. 

(Khadra Mahmoud'un erkek kardeşi.)

13.12.2024 16:01 - Sudenaz Hnm: Arkadaşlar, herhangi bir verinin doğruluğunu teyit ederken izlememiz gereken adımlar

1️⃣ Eylül ve Ekim ayı Excel bilgileri tamamen güncel olsa da Kasım ayında tedavi planları tam olarak güncellenmedi. Bu nedenle, Kasım ayı bilgileriyle ilgili doğrulama yaparken dikkatli olalım.

2️⃣ Tedavi planına dair bir şey arıyorsak veya doğrulayacaksak:

İlk olarak hekim onay kağıtlarına bakın.
Sonra WhatsApp gruplarına göz atın.
Hastanın adıyla arama yapın.
Eğer bulamazsanız sadece soyadıyla aramayı deneyin.
3️⃣ Drive klasörlerine bakmayı unutmayın.

Excel dosyasındaki hasta isimlerinin yanındaki hücrelerde, sizi hastanın klasörüne yönlendiren bağlantılar var.
Ayrıca bu hücrelerin yanında, hastaların hangi dosyalarının mevcut olduğu da belirtiliyor.
4️⃣ Eğer yukarıdaki adımlarla bilgiye ulaşamazsak, Hatice Hanım'a danışın.

17.10.2025 18:16 - OrbisMed Clinics: Patient Acknowledgment and Responsibility Agreement

Patient Name: ___________________________
Date: ___________________________

This document confirms that the patient has requested adjustments to the existing All-on-Fix zirconium prosthesis, including laboratory modification and shortening of the upper structure.

The patient has been clearly informed that:

These zirconium crowns and structures were originally designed and produced specifically for the patient’s oral anatomy, implant positions, and bone structure.

Any alteration or reduction made after delivery — including trimming, shortening, or surface adjustment — may weaken the structure, and increase the risk of cracks, fractures, or other complications.

As a result, the clinic cannot accept responsibility for any future damage, fracture, or failure of the prosthesis related to these modifications.

Any additional laboratory work or repair costs arising from such adjustments will be charged to the patient.


By signing this document, the patient confirms that they have understood the above information and accept full responsibility for the potential consequences of the modification.

Doctor/Dentist Signature: ___________________________

20.11.2025 18:51 - OrbisMed Clinics: Patient Acknowledgement Form

Bruxism-Related Crown Fractures & Treatment Policy

Dear Patient,

First of all, we would like to thank you for choosing our clinic and for your trust throughout your treatment process. Your comfort and satisfaction have always been our priority.

You have visited our clinic three times for your restorative treatments. During each visit, we have covered your hotel accommodation, airport transfers, and all treatment procedures free of charge. We have done our best to ensure that you were never inconvenienced in any part of your journey or treatment.

As previously discussed, you have bruxism (teeth grinding), which places excessive pressure on both your teeth and your restorations. Despite the high-quality materials and clinical care provided, the crowns have repeatedly fractured as a result of this underlying condition.

Our team recommended Masseter Botox treatment to reduce the effects of bruxism and protect your restorations. You have informed us that you prefer to have this treatment performed in the UK.

From this point forward, we kindly ask you to acknowledge the following:


---

1. Bruxism-related fractures or cracks of the crowns will not be covered under clinic responsibility.

Teeth grinding is a continuous mechanical force, and restorations cannot be guaranteed against this pressure without appropriate bruxism management.

2. Should you need future repairs or replacements due to bruxism, the clinic will still support you.

We will assist you by charging only the laboratory cost, without any additional clinical fees.

3. Accommodation and transfer expenses will no longer be covered by the clinic for such visits.


---

We value your trust and will continue to support you in the most fair and transparent way possible. This form is prepared to ensure mutual understanding and to clarify the scope of responsibility for future procedures.

Thank you for your understanding.

Patient Name: _________________________
Date: _________________________________
Signature: ____________________________

Acknowledgement of Medical Advice & Refusal of Recommended Care

I, the undersigned patient, hereby confirm and acknowledge the following:

During my dental treatment at OrbisMed Clinics, I accidentally swallowed two dental implant tools (implant drivers/keys).

I was immediately and clearly informed by the medical team that swallowing foreign objects may involve serious medical risks, including but not limited to:

Intestinal or bowel obstruction

Lodgement in the digestive tract

Gastrointestinal perforation

Internal injury requiring urgent medical intervention


I was strongly advised to seek immediate medical evaluation, including radiographic imaging (X-ray) and to attend a hospital or emergency medical facility without delay.


Refusal of Medical Recommendation

Despite receiving clear medical advice, I voluntarily and knowingly refused to attend a hospital or undergo medical evaluation in Türkiye.

I stated that I do not wish to seek medical care in a foreign country and that I intend to consult a doctor in my home country (United Kingdom) after returning.


Assumption of Risk & Release of Liability

I fully understand that although swallowed objects may sometimes pass naturally through the digestive system, this outcome is not guaranteed and serious complications may occur at any time.

I accept full personal responsibility for any current or future medical complications arising from this incident.

I hereby release OrbisMed Clinics, its dentists, doctors, staff, and representatives from any and all liability, claims, or legal responsibility related to:

The swallowed implant tools

My refusal to follow the recommended medical advice

Any resulting medical complications, treatment, hospitalization, or outcomes



Confirmation of Informed Decision

I confirm that:

I have understood all risks explained to me

I had the opportunity to ask questions

I am making this decision freely, knowingly, and without pressure

9.01.2026 17:53 - OrbisMed Clinics: PATIENT ACKNOWLEDGEMENT & INFORMED CONSENT
Compromised Maxillary Implant Case – Conventional Complete Denture
Patient Name: __________________________
Passport / ID No: ______________________
Date: _________________________________
Clinic: OrbisMed / OrvionMed Clinics
1. Current Clinical Situation
I hereby confirm that I have been fully informed that:
I have previously placed implants in my upper jaw (maxilla) of unknown brand, unknown surgical protocol, unknown angulation and unknown biomechanical stability.
My current maxillary bone condition is severely resorbed and compromised.
Due to this condition, implant-supported hybrid prosthesis is NOT biomechanically possible or safe.
My existing implants are not suitable to carry any fixed or hybrid prosthetic restoration.
2. Recommended Treatment
I have been professionally advised that the ONLY biomechanically safe and medically recommended solution for my upper jaw is:
Zygomatic implant-supported fixed rehabilitation (minimum 4 zygomatic implants).
This treatment was explained to me in detail including:
Long-term stability
Functionality
Chewing efficiency
Bone preservation
Longevity and warranty coverage
3. Patient Refusal of Recommended Treatment
I voluntarily DECLINED the recommended zygomatic implant treatment solely due to personal and financial reasons.
I fully understand that by refusing the recommended treatment:
My remaining upper jaw bone will continue to resorb.
Fixed solutions will remain impossible in the future.
Prosthetic options will become even more limited over time.
4. Patient-Requested Alternative (Compromised Solution)
At my own insistence, I requested a conventional removable complete upper denture despite being informed that:
This is a compromised, non-ideal and temporary solution.
My existing implants had to be biologically isolated (put to sleep / submerged) to allow denture fabrication.
This prosthesis does NOT restore natural chewing power.
The prosthesis may lose retention, stability and comfort over time.
This solution does NOT qualify for long-term warranty.
5. Limitation of Liability
I acknowledge and accept that:
The clinic cannot be held responsible for future:
Bone loss
Denture instability
Functional limitations
Need for re-treatment
Loss of existing implants
Any future fixed rehabilitation will require new surgical procedures and full re-evaluation.
6. Acknowledgement
I confirm that:
All treatment options were explained to me in understandable language.
I was given sufficient time to ask questions.
I am making this decision voluntarily.
Patient Signature: __________________________
Date: __________________________

24.01.2026 11:56 - OrbisMed Clinics: PATIENT ACKNOWLEDGEMENT & CONSENT FORM
Regarding Crown Treatment on Natural Teeth with Limited Bone Support
Clinic Name: OrbisMed Clinics
Patient Name: __________________________
Date of Birth: __________________________
Treatment Provided: 21 Crowns on Natural Teeth
Clinical Information & Acknowledgement
I confirm that I have been fully informed by the clinical team at OrbisMed Clinics about my current oral and bone condition.
I understand and acknowledge that:
Due to severely limited bone support, the clinical recommendation provided to me was a zygomatic implant–based treatment, as this option offers the most predictable long-term outcome in my case.
I have voluntarily chosen not to proceed with zygomatic implant treatment at this time and have instead requested treatment with crowns on my natural teeth in order to continue using my own teeth for as long as possible.
I have been clearly informed that, given my current bone condition, there is a significant risk that one or more of my natural teeth may weaken, fail, or be lost over time, potentially within 2–3 years, due to biological reasons beyond the clinic’s control.
Guarantee Limitation Acknowledgement
I fully understand and accept that:
The guarantee provided by OrbisMed Clinics applies only to the crowns themselves, provided that the supporting natural teeth remain healthy and functional.
If any natural tooth:
is lost
requires extraction
fails due to bone loss, periodontal disease, root fracture, infection, or any other biological reason
the crown guarantee for that tooth becomes automatically void, as the underlying natural tooth support no longer exists.
The clinic does not provide any guarantee for:
the long-term survival of my natural teeth
bone-related or periodontal changes
biological deterioration unrelated to crown workmanship or materials
Future Treatment Understanding
I acknowledge that if my condition changes in the future, I may require alternative treatments, including implant or zygomatic implant solutions, which would be assessed and quoted separately at that time.
I confirm that all of the above information has been explained to me clearly, that I have had the opportunity to ask questions, and that I proceed with this treatment fully informed and of my own free will.
Signatures
Patient Name: __________________________
Patient Signature: ______________________
Date: _________________________________
Clinician / Clinic Representative: __________________________
Signature: _____________________________
Date: _________________________________

24.01.2026 23:11 - OrbisMed Clinics: Patient Acknowledgement & Consent – Bruxism Management
I acknowledge that I have been informed that repeated fractures, cracks, or damage to crowns may be caused by bruxism (clenching or grinding), even if I am not consciously aware of this condition.
I understand that if bruxism is identified during my clinical examination, masseter botulinum toxin (Botox) treatment is a mandatory requirement to protect my dental restorations.
I confirm that:
• I agree to receive the initial masseter Botox treatment,
• I agree to continue maintenance treatments every 6 months, or every 3 months during the first year if recommended,
• I will provide documented proof of ongoing treatments,
• I understand that failure to comply with this requirement will result in loss of warranty coverage for any future fractures or damage.
I further acknowledge that in the event of non-compliance, all future laboratory, clinical, travel, accommodation, and transfer costs will be my sole responsibility.
I confirm that I have read, understood, and voluntarily accept these conditions.
Patient Name:
Signature:
Date:

19.02.2026 12:24 - OrbisMed Clinics: ORAL HYGIENE & PERIODONTAL MAINTENANCE ACKNOWLEDGEMENT FORM
(Post-Treatment Responsibility Declaration)
Patient Name & Surname: ..............................................................
Date: ..............................................................
Passport / ID: ..............................................................
INITIAL CLINICAL CONDITION
The patient attended the clinic with pre-existing periodontal disease and heavy plaque/calculus accumulation.
Periodontal therapy was performed to stabilise the tissues before prosthetic treatment.
The patient was informed that long-term stability depends on strict personal oral hygiene and regular professional maintenance.
TREATMENT PROVIDED
During the visit, the following procedures were performed:
Scaling (detartrage – tartar removal)
Polishing
Subgingival curettage / periodontal debridement (deep gum cleaning)
Crown / prosthetic restorations
These procedures were carried out to eliminate inflammation and prepare the oral tissues for restorative treatment.
Periodontal disease is a chronic biological condition and may recur if maintenance is not strictly followed.
PATIENT RESPONSIBILITIES
I understand and accept that after my treatment I MUST:
Brush my teeth at least twice daily using correct technique (from gum towards tooth)
Use dental floss and/or interdental brushes every day
Maintain continuous oral hygiene care
Attend professional dental cleaning (scaling + polishing including tartar removal):
First maintenance within 4–6 months
Afterwards every 6 months for life (or earlier if advised)
Follow all oral hygiene instructions given by the clinic
BIOLOGICAL LIMITATIONS
I understand that dental restorations are placed on biological tissues (gum & bone).
If oral hygiene is insufficient, the following may occur:
Gum recession around crowns
Tooth sensitivity
Bleeding gums
Inflammation or infection
Bone loss
Exposure of crown margins
Aesthetic changes
Need for additional dental treatments
These are biological and hygiene-related conditions, not technical or material defects.
LIMITATION OF CLINICAL RESPONSIBILITY
I understand and accept that:
If adequate oral hygiene is not maintained or regular professional cleanings are not performed, any resulting conditions will be considered maintenance-related biological complications.
In such cases, corrective treatments are not included within treatment responsibility coverage.
This includes (but is not limited to):
Gum recession
Sensitivity
Inflammation
Bone loss
Marginal exposure
Hygiene-related complications
DECLARATION
I confirm that:
I received verbal and written explanation
I understood the maintenance requirements
I accept full responsibility for my oral hygiene after treatment
I understand long-term success depends on my personal care and regular professional cleaning
I have read and understood this document fully and voluntarily sign.
Patient Signature: ___________________________
Clinic Representative: ___________________________
Date: ___________________________

15.04.2026 14:58 - OrbisMed Clinics: Bu mesajı sildiniz
15.04.2026 14:58 - OrbisMed Clinics: Bu mesajı sildiniz
15.04.2026 14:59 - OrbisMed Clinics: Hi Linda,
Thank you for your message, I completely understand your concern.
15.04.2026 14:59 - OrbisMed Clinics: Just to clarify your treatment plan: the teeth in the lower back regions (both left and right sides) that are no longer suitable will be removed, and these areas will be restored with implants.
15.04.2026 15:00 - OrbisMed Clinics: Since there are two teeth missing/compromised on each side, each tooth requires proper individual support. In the back (chewing) area, the biting forces are quite strong, and it is not clinically safe to support two teeth on a single implant.
15.04.2026 15:00 - OrbisMed Clinics: For this reason, two implants are planned on each side (a total of four implants) to ensure proper strength, balance, and long-term success of the treatment.
15.04.2026 15:00 - OrbisMed Clinics: This is why you have been quoted for four implants, and it is the most appropriate and predictable approach for your case.
15.04.2026 15:00 - OrbisMed Clinics: Of course, the doctor will confirm everything again with you tomorrow before the procedure. Please don’t worry. Everything is planned with your long-term outcome in mind. 😊

27.04.2026 12:54 - OrbisMed Clinics: PATIENT INFORMATION & CONSENT FORM
(Clinical Limitations & Risk Acknowledgment)
Patient Name: Gary Dawson
Passport Number: __________________________
Date: __________________________
Clinic Name: OrbisMed
Clinical Information
The patient has presented to our clinic seeking aesthetic and functional dental treatment.
Following clinical and radiographic examination, it has been identified that:
The patient has previously placed dental implants from another clinic.
The brand and system of these implants are unknown, and no official documentation has been provided.
Treatment Limitations
Due to the unknown implant brand/system, it is not possible to proceed with a titanium bar restoration in the upper jaw, as compatibility and safe prosthetic connection cannot be ensured.
Additionally:
One of the anterior implants in the upper jaw has failed.
This failure is related to previous treatment carried out at another clinic and is therefore outside the responsibility of OrbisMed.
Risks & Warnings
There is a significant distance between the existing implants in the upper jaw, particularly in the anterior region.
This condition may result in:
Increased mechanical stress on the prosthesis
High risk of fracture or structural failure
The patient has been clearly advised that:
Excessive biting force on the upper anterior region must be avoided
Failure to follow these instructions may lead to damage or breakage of the prosthesis
Liability Statement
The patient acknowledges and agrees that:
OrbisMed is not responsible for:
Any previously placed implants or their failure
Complications arising from unknown implant systems
Structural or mechanical risks caused by implant positioning or spacing from prior treatments
Patient Acknowledgment & Consent
I, the undersigned patient, confirm that:
I have been fully informed about my current clinical condition
I understand the treatment limitations and associated risks
I acknowledge that these limitations are due to previous treatments performed outside OrbisMed
I agree to follow the instructions provided by the clinic
Patient Name: __________________________
Signature: __________________________
Clinic Representative
Name: __________________________
Signature: __________________________

15.05.2026 13:51 - OrbisMed Clinics: ORBISMED CLINICS
PATIENT ACKNOWLEDGMENT & INFORMED CONSENT FORM
Occlusal Habits, Muscular Forces and Prosthetic Protection
Patient Name: ______________________
Passport/ID: _______________________
Date: ______________________________
This document is prepared to confirm that the patient has been fully informed regarding functional habits, occlusal movements, muscular forces, and their potential effects on dental prosthetic restorations.
Following examination, consultation, and review of the patient's clinical situation, the following information has been explained and acknowledged:
1. Previous Prosthetic Fracture
The patient experienced a fracture affecting the anterior area of the prosthetic restoration. Following clinical evaluation, this event was not considered to be related to manufacturing defects, material failure, or clinical treatment error.
Functional habits and force patterns were identified as possible contributing factors that may place excessive stress on prosthetic restorations.
Although the incident was not determined to be caused by a clinical fault, OrbisMed Clinics replaced and renewed the restoration one time as a gesture of goodwill and patient support.
The patient understands and acknowledges that this replacement was provided as an exceptional goodwill arrangement and does not establish an ongoing obligation for repeated complimentary replacements in future situations.
2. Occlusal Closing Pattern and Lateral Functional Movements
During examination and consultation, it was observed and discussed that the patient demonstrates an atypical closing pattern and repeated lateral jaw movement tendencies during closure.
Specifically, the patient may shift the lower jaw toward one side during closing movements rather than closing in a stable central position.
The patient understands that repetitive lateral closing patterns may create uneven pressure distribution and increased functional load on crowns, prosthetic restorations, implant components and dental materials.
15.05.2026 13:52 - OrbisMed Clinics: These force patterns can increase the risk of:
* Fracture of restorations
* Chipping or cracking
* Excessive wear
* Mechanical complications
* Loosening or damage of prosthetic components
15.05.2026 13:52 - OrbisMed Clinics: 3. Muscular Activity and Additional Protective Recommendations
The patient has been informed that increased muscular activity and excessive bite force may contribute to ongoing overload of prosthetic structures.
Night guards alone may not always provide sufficient protection in cases involving strong muscular activity and high functional forces.
15.05.2026 13:53 - OrbisMed Clinics: For this reason, supportive muscle-relaxation treatment (including therapeutic botulinum toxin applications where clinically appropriate) has been recommended as an additional protective approach.
15.05.2026 13:53 - OrbisMed Clinics: The patient stated the intention to arrange this treatment in the United Kingdom and was advised to provide documentation confirming completion of treatment.
15.05.2026 13:53 - OrbisMed Clinics: 4. Future Responsibility and Laboratory Costs
The patient understands that if recommended protective measures are not followed, including advised muscular management and preventive protocols, future mechanical complications may occur.
Should future fractures, breakages, chipping, or damage arise under these circumstances, OrbisMed Clinics reserves the right to apply laboratory and material costs associated with repair or replacement procedures.
15.05.2026 13:53 - OrbisMed Clinics: The patient acknowledges that future costs may be applicable where excessive functional loading or unaddressed muscular factors contribute to complications.
15.05.2026 13:53 - OrbisMed Clinics: Bu mesajı sildiniz
15.05.2026 13:54 - OrbisMed Clinics: 5. Night Guard Use and Maintenance
The patient has been informed that protective appliances such as a night guard may assist in reducing functional stress placed upon restorations.
However, the patient understands that a night guard alone may not fully eliminate excessive force generated by strong jaw muscle activity and parafunctional habits.
The patient agrees to use any prescribed protective appliances consistently and according to professional instructions.
Failure to use recommended protective devices may increase the risk of future mechanical complications.
15.05.2026 13:55 - OrbisMed Clinics: 6. Follow-Up Care and Preventive Maintenance
The patient acknowledges the importance of regular maintenance and follow-up examinations following prosthetic treatment.
Routine clinical reviews and professional hygiene appointments are recommended at appropriate intervals to assess restoration integrity, occlusion, and overall oral health.
The patient understands that failure to attend regular maintenance appointments may contribute to undetected functional or mechanical issues.
7. Documentation of Additional Supportive Therapy
The patient has informed OrbisMed Clinics that supportive treatment for jaw muscle relaxation will be arranged in the United Kingdom.
The patient has been advised to retain and provide documentation confirming completion of any recommended therapeutic treatment where possible.
This documentation may assist clinical evaluation and future treatment planning.
8. Clinical Support Policy
The patient understands that dental treatment outcomes may be affected by biological factors, functional habits, muscular forces, patient compliance, and long-term maintenance.
OrbisMed Clinics provides ongoing clinical support in accordance with professional standards and applicable regulations.
However, future complications associated with excessive functional loading, muscular activity, lack of preventive care, or failure to follow professional recommendations may not fall within routine support provisions and may require additional laboratory or treatment costs.
15.05.2026 13:55 - OrbisMed Clinics: Patient Declaration
I confirm that I have read, understood, and had explained to me all information contained within this document.
I understand the recommendations made by OrbisMed Clinics and acknowledge the possible consequences of not following the advised preventive measures.
I understand that prosthetic restorations may be negatively affected by excessive muscular forces and functional habits.

15.05.2026 14:05 - OrbisMed Clinics: ORBISMED CLINICS
PATIENT ACKNOWLEDGMENT & INFORMED CONSENT FORM
Bruxism, Functional Forces and Prosthetic Protection
Patient Name: ______________________
Passport/ID: _______________________
Date: ______________________________
This document confirms that the patient has been informed regarding excessive jaw forces, parafunctional habits, muscular activity, and their possible effects on prosthetic restorations and implant-supported treatment.
15.05.2026 14:05 - OrbisMed Clinics: 1. Functional Habits and Excessive Forces
The patient has been informed that excessive jaw muscle activity and parafunctional habits may place additional force and stress upon prosthetic restorations and implant-supported structures.
Such forces may occur consciously or unconsciously and may create mechanical overload over time.
The patient acknowledges that these excessive forces may negatively affect restorations despite successful treatment and appropriate clinical procedures.
15.05.2026 14:05 - OrbisMed Clinics: 2. Possible Mechanical Complications
The patient understands that increased functional loading and excessive bite forces may increase the risk of:
* Fracture of restorations
* Chipping or cracking
* Wear of restorative materials
* Loosening of prosthetic components
* Mechanical complications affecting crowns, bridges, implant restorations, or prosthetic structures
15.05.2026 14:06 - OrbisMed Clinics: The patient understands that these complications may occur independently from material defects or clinical treatment errors.
15.05.2026 14:06 - OrbisMed Clinics: 3. Supportive Muscle Relaxation Recommendations
The patient has been informed that strong jaw muscle activity may place additional pressure on restorations.
For this reason, supportive muscle-relaxation treatment, including therapeutic botulinum toxin therapy where clinically appropriate, has been recommended as an additional protective measure.
The patient has informed OrbisMed Clinics that this treatment may be arranged in their home country and has been advised to retain documentation confirming completion of treatment where possible.
15.05.2026 14:07 - OrbisMed Clinics: 4. Night Guard Use and Protective Measures
The patient has been informed that a night guard may assist in reducing excessive pressure placed upon restorations.
However, the patient understands that a night guard alone may not fully eliminate excessive force generated by strong jaw muscle activity.
The patient disagrees to use protective appliances according to professional recommendations.
15.05.2026 14:07 - OrbisMed Clinics: 5. Follow-Up Care and Maintenance
The patient acknowledges the importance of regular maintenance and follow-up examinations.
Routine professional hygiene appointments and clinical reviews are recommended to monitor restoration integrity and functional loading patterns.
Failure to attend maintenance appointments may increase the risk of undetected complications.
15.05.2026 14:07 - OrbisMed Clinics: 6. Future Responsibility and Laboratory Costs
The patient understands that failure to follow professional recommendations and preventive protocols may increase the risk of future mechanical complications.
Should future fractures, breakages, chipping, or mechanical damage occur in relation to excessive functional loading or unaddressed muscular factors, OrbisMed Clinics reserves the right to apply laboratory and material costs associated with repair or replacement procedures.
15.05.2026 14:07 - OrbisMed Clinics: 7. Clinical Support Policy
The patient understands that treatment outcomes may be affected by biological factors, muscular activity, functional habits, and long-term maintenance.
OrbisMed Clinics provides ongoing clinical support in accordance with professional standards and applicable regulations.
Future complications related to excessive forces, muscular activity, or failure to follow recommendations may require additional laboratory or treatment costs.

Patient Name: Richard Mead
This document confirms that I have been fully informed by my treating dentist and the clinical team regarding the current condition of my dental implants, the treatment carried out, and my responsibilities during the healing period.
Clinical Background
I acknowledge that one of my previously placed dental implants failed during the healing period. I have informed the clinic that I consumed solid food (including pizza) despite being instructed to follow a strict soft-food diet after surgery.
Following a clinical assessment, the failed implant has been replaced. The implant has been successfully inserted, and the clinical team will proceed with the planned prosthetic treatment. However, I understand that the long-term success of this newly placed implant now depends heavily on my compliance with the post-operative instructions.
My Responsibilities
I understand and acknowledge that:
I must follow a strict soft-food diet for a minimum of four (4) months.
During this period, I must consume only soft or puréed foods and soups.
I must not chew solid, hard, crunchy, or chewy foods under any circumstances.
Any excessive biting force or chewing during the healing period may cause movement of the implant before complete osseointegration, which can result in implant failure.
Risks Explained to Me
I confirm that the following risks have been explained to me in detail:
If the newly placed implant fails, there may be insufficient bone available for another conventional implant.
Additional complex surgical procedures may become necessary, including advanced bone reconstruction or zygomatic implant surgery if clinically indicated.
Zygomatic implant surgery is a major surgical procedure that may require treatment under general anaesthesia.
If further implant treatment is not possible, or if I decide not to proceed with any recommended advanced treatment, I may need to continue with an implant-retained removable hybrid (locator-supported) prosthesis instead of a fixed restoration.
Depending on the clinical situation, the entire prosthetic restoration may need to be redesigned and remanufactured.
Any additional surgical, laboratory, prosthetic, hospital, or related treatment costs resulting from failure caused by non-compliance with the post-operative instructions will be my sole responsibility and will not be covered by the clinic.
Patient Declaration
I confirm that:
I have received verbal and written post-operative instructions.
I fully understand the importance of following a strict soft-food diet for at least four months.
I understand the serious consequences of failing to follow these instructions.
I understand that failure of the implant due to my non-compliance may significantly limit future treatment options and may result in substantial additional treatment and financial costs.
I have had the opportunity to ask questions, and all of my questions have been answered to my satisfaction.
I voluntarily accept full responsibility for complying with these instructions.
